# THE EXAM DOCKET — Deuteronomy 14:1-29 (the sons and the cuttings — sons you are to the LORD your God, you shall not cut yourselves nor make baldness between your eyes for the dead, a holy people chosen to be His treasured people; the beasts — you shall not eat any abomination, the ox, the sheep and the goat, the seven wild beasts, every beast that parts the hoof cloven in two and chews the cud, the camel, the hare, the coney and the swine, their carcass you shall not touch; the water and the birds — all that have fins and scales, every clean bird you may eat, the twenty-one named unclean, every swarming winged thing, every clean fowl; the carcass and the kid — you shall not eat any carcass, to the sojourner in your gates give it or sell it to a foreigner, you shall not boil a kid in its mother's milk; the second tithe — tithe, you shall tithe all the yield of your seed year by year, eat before the LORD in the place which He will choose the tithe of your grain, wine and oil and the firstlings of your herd and flock, that you may learn to fear; the far place, the money, the rejoicing and the Levite — when the way is too long turn it into money, bind up the money in your hand, spend it for whatever your soul desires, rejoice, you and your household, the Levite in your gates you shall not forsake; the third year — at the end of three years bring out all the tithe of your yield and lay it up within your gates, the Levite, the sojourner, the fatherless and the widow shall come and eat and be satisfied, that the LORD may bless you) — THE DEUTERONOMY WALK sitting 12b, THE DOCKET IN TWO RUNS (2026-09-21; DEUTERONOMY_WALK.md "Sitting 12b — THE COMPILE OF CHAPTER 14 … THE DESIGN", THE ORDER; its "THE DOCKET — AS RUN (D1)" and "(D2)" paragraphs)

## Mishnah Maaser Sheni
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 1:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. second tithe not sold, not pledged, not exchanged, not used as a weight; not 'here is wine, give me oil' even in Jerusalem; a free gift permitted: F5 — second_tithe_owed's SANCTITY (Heaven's property — Kiddushin 54b at D2): the four bars; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 1:2 [TOPIC] — LAW. the cattle tithe not sold whole and alive nor blemished; the firstborn sold; no betrothal with the tithe; SECOND TITHE NOT REDEEMED WITH UNSTAMPED COINS, nor uncurrent coins, nor money not in one's possession: F6 — the_moneys_form's ANSWER SHEET — coined, current, possessed (107:4-5); temurah.tithe by CALL for the cattle tithe's sale; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 1:3 [TOPIC] — LAW. a domestic animal bought for a peace offering or a wild one for common eating — the hide common though worth more than the flesh; sealed jars where sold sealed — common; nut shells common; grape-skin wine before fermenting not bought, after — bought: F6 — THE CONTAINERS (107:12-15) at the answer sheet; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 1:4 [TOPIC] — LAW. a wild animal for a peace offering or a domestic one for common eating — the hide not common; open jars where sold open — not common; baskets of olives or grapes with the vessel — the vessel's value not common: F6 — the containers' inverse; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 1:5 [TOPIC] — LAW. water, salt, produce joined to the soil, produce that cannot reach Jerusalem — no purchase; unwitting — the money returned; deliberate — taken up and eaten in the place; NO TEMPLE — LEFT TO ROT: F6 — THE CLASS'S EDGE (107:8-11 — water and salt out) and THE EXILE'S ARM (the third state, as chapter 12's slaughter law); the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 1:6 [TOPIC] — LAW. a domestic animal bought unwittingly — the money returned; deliberately — eaten in the place; no Temple — buried with its hide: F6 — the exile's arm on the animal; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 1:7 [TOPIC] — LAW. no slaves, land or unclean animals with tithe money — bought, their value eaten; no bird offerings of the impure, no sin or guilt offerings — their value eaten; THE GENERAL RULE: what is not eating, drinking or anointing — its value eaten: F6 — the class's general rule (107:8-11); the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. second tithe for eating, drinking and anointing — each what is usual; no anointing with wine or vinegar, oil yes; no spiced tithe oil, spiced wine yes; honey or spices fallen into wine — the improvement divided by proportion; baked dough — all tithe; the rule: a recognizable improvement by proportion: F6 — the three uses and the improvement's rule; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:2 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Shimon: no anointing with tithe oil in Jerusalem; the Sages allow — the terumah argument and his answer (vetches and fenugreek): F6 — the anointing's arms; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:3 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. fenugreek of second tithe eaten while tender; of terumah — Beit Shammai in purity except the head's cleansing, Beit Hillel in impurity except the soaking: F6 — the tithe's clause plain; the terumah's dispute OUTSIDE; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:4 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. vetches of second tithe eaten tender, brought in and out of Jerusalem; if impure — R. Tarfon: divided among dough, the Sages: redeemed; the terumah's houses and Shammai and R. Akiva: F6 — the impure tithe's remedy (R. Tarfon / the Sages); the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:5 [TOPIC] — LAW. common and tithe coins scattered together — what is picked one by one is tithe until the sum is complete, the rest common; taken by the handful — by proportion: F6 — the money's mixture rule; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:6 [TOPIC] — LAW. a tithe sela mixed with a common sela — copper coins brought, the tithe sela declared onto them, the better sela chosen and the coppers exchanged back: silver for copper only in need: F6 — the money's exchange rite; the exam's row.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:7 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. Beit Shammai: no turning selas into gold dinars; Beit Hillel permit; R. Akiva did it for Rabban Gamliel and R. Yehoshua: F6 — the_moneys_form's EXCHANGE ARM (Bava Metzia 45a:6's ground); the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:8 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. copper coins of tithe for a sela — Beit Shammai the whole sela; Beit Hillel half silver, half copper; R. Meir: not silver and produce for silver, the Sages allow: F6 — the exchange outside the place; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:9 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. a tithe sela exchanged in Jerusalem — Beit Shammai all copper; Beit Hillel half; the disciples three dinars and one; R. Akiva three and a quarter of the fourth; R. Tarfon four aspers; Shammai: leave it in a shop and eat its worth: F6 — the exchange in the place, five arms; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 2:10 [TOPIC] — LAW. some sons pure, some impure — a sela set down: what the pure drink is redeemed onto it; both drink from one jar: F6 — the redemption's declaration for drinkers; the exam's row.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. not 'carry this produce up to Jerusalem to divide it' but 'that we may eat and drink of it there'; a gift permitted: F5 — the sanctity — no payment for carrying (1:1's twin); the exam's row.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:2 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. no terumah bought with tithe money — fewer eaters; R. Shimon permits — the peace-offering argument and the Sages' answer: F6 — the purchase of terumah (R. Shimon / the Sages); the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:3 [TOPIC] — LAW. tithe money in Jerusalem and a friend's common produce — 'let this money be exchanged for your produce': the friend eats his in purity, the owner spends his money; not to an unlearned man (am ha-aretz) except from demai (doubtfully tithed) money: F6 — the money's transfer in the place; the exam's row.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:4 [TOPIC] — LAW. common produce in Jerusalem and tithe money in the provinces, or the reverse — 'let that money be exchanged for this produce', the produce carried up and eaten there: F6 — the declaration across the distance; the exam's row.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:5 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. tithe money brought in and out of Jerusalem; tithe produce in but not out — Rabban Shimon ben Gamliel: out too: F6 — THE WALL ABSORBS THE PRODUCE (Mishnah Zevachim 5:8's wall — 106:3): the arms; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:6 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. finished produce that passed through Jerusalem — its tithe brought back and eaten there; unfinished (grapes for the press, figs for drying) — Beit Shammai back, Beit Hillel redeemed anywhere; R. Shimon ben Yehuda in R. Yose's name narrows the dispute to finished produce; demai (doubtfully tithed) in and out and redeemed: F6 — the wall's absorption by the processing's state; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:7 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. a tree inside bending out or outside bending in — what faces the wall inward is inside; olive presses with the door in and the space out — Beit Shammai all inside, Beit Hillel by the wall's line: F5 — THE WALL (106:3) at the answer sheet, its line drawn on trees and presses; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:8 [TOPIC — CREDITED: deu_06_vaetchanan_2026-09-17.md, deu_06_vaetchanan_exam_2026-09-17.md] — LAW. - Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:9 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. second tithe that entered Jerusalem and became impure, by a primary or a secondary source, inside or outside — Beit Shammai: all redeemed and eaten inside except the primary outside; Beit Hillel: all redeemed and eaten outside except the secondary inside: F6 — the impure tithe's redemption (Pesachim 36b:4's R. Eliezer) at the answer sheet; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:10 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. what was bought with tithe money became impure — redeemed; R. Yehuda: buried (the tithe itself is redeemable pure at a distance, the purchase not): F6 — the purchase's impurity (the Sages / R. Yehuda); the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:11 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. a deer bought with tithe money that died — buried with its hide (R. Shimon: redeemed); bought alive, slaughtered and impure — redeemed (R. Yose: buried); bought slaughtered and impure — as produce: F6 — the purchased animal's three cases, two disputes; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:12 [TOPIC] — LAW. jars lent for tithe wine — corked or not, no sanctity; wine poured in unspecified — sanctity once corked; before corking neutralized in a hundred and one, after — any quantity sanctifies; terumah from one jar for all before, per jar after: F6 — the containers' sanctity by the cork (107:12-15); the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 3:13 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. Beit Shammai: the jars opened and emptied into the press; Beit Hillel: opened, not emptied; where sold open the jar stays tithe — sold by measure it reverts (R. Shimon: 'from its jar' reverts): F6 — the jar's reversion; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. produce carried from a cheap place to a dear one or the reverse — redeemed at the price of the place of redemption; from the threshing floor or press to the city — the increase the tithe's, the expenses the household's: F6 — THE REDEMPTION'S PRICE by the place (14:25's turning into money at the answer sheet); the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:2 [TOPIC] — LAW. redeemed at the lower market price — as the shopkeeper buys, as the changer takes; not in an estimated lump; a known value by one assessor, unknown (filmed wine, rotting produce, rusted coins) by three: F6 — the assessors' rule; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:3 [TOPIC] — LAW. the owner's 'a sela' and another's 'a sela' — the owner first, for he adds a fifth; another's 'a sela and an issar (a small coin)' first — he added to the principal; THE FIFTH added by the owner, his own or a gift: F6 — THE ADDED FIFTH (Leviticus 27:31 by CALL temurah.redeem); the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:4 [TOPIC] — LAW. the fifth avoided lawfully — 'take this money and redeem it for yourself' to a grown son or daughter or a Hebrew slave; not to a minor or a Canaanite slave, whose hand is his: F6 — the fifth's evasion by another's hand; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:5 [TOPIC] — LAW. at the threshing floor without coins — 'this produce is given you as a gift', then 'let it be exchanged for money I have at home': F6 — the redemption without coins at hand (107:5's possession edge); the exam's row.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:6 [TOPIC] — LAW. possession taken for a sela, the price rose to two — one sela paid, the profit his; taken for two, fell to one — one common sela and one of tithe money; an unlearned owner — from demai (doubtfully tithed): F6 — the price's change before the redemption; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:7 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. redeemed without naming it — R. Yose: sufficient; R. Yehuda: he must name it (the divorce and betrothal twin): F6 — the redemption's declaration; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:8 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. an issar set aside, half eaten against it, then a place where it is worth a pondion — another issar's worth eaten; the reverse; the leftover fraction — Beit Shammai a tenth, Beit Hillel an eleventh for certain tithe and a tenth for demai (doubtfully tithed): F6 — the coin's value by place and the leftover's fraction; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:9 [TOPIC] — LAW. found money is common, even gold dinars with silver and copper; a potsherd marked 'tithe' with it — tithe money: F6 — the found money's status; the exam's rows.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:10 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. a vessel marked 'korban' (an offering) — R. Yehuda: clay — the vessel common, its contents holy; metal — the vessel holy, its contents common; the Sages: none puts the common into the holy: F6 — the marked vessel (the tithe's marks follow at 4:11); a DATA row; the exam's arm.
- Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:11 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. the letters: kof — korban (offering), mem — maaser (tithe), dalet — demai (doubtful), tet — tevel (untithed), tav — terumah (in the danger they wrote tav); R. - Mishnah Maaser Sheni 4:12 [TOPIC] — LAW. 'tithe in this corner' and found in another — common; a hundred said and two hundred found — the rest common; two hundred said and a hundred found — all tithe: F6 — the father's word on found produce; the exam's rows.

Yehuda: does a substitute make a substitute?); called ninth tenth, tenth tenth, eleventh tenth — the eleventh not sanctified; THE RULE: unless the name 'tenth' was uprooted from the tenth, the eleventh is not sanctified. Elazar b. R. Tzadok: the bird that splits its digits on a string unclean: F3 — THE_BIRDS_SIGNS' ANSWER SHEET (Mishnah Chullin 3:6 — 103:8, the design's parameter) AT ITS FOLIO: the four signs and the string; the exam's row.

Yehuda's arm a DATA row; the exam's row.
- Chullin 59a:8 [TOPIC] — LAW. the baraita: the clean beast's signs — 'whatever parts the hoof, is wholly cloven and chews the cud' (Leviticus 11:3); whatever chews the cud has no upper incisors and is clean: F2 — shemini.classify by CALL (the signs; 14:6 the twin); the teeth's mark a DATA row; the exam's row.
- Chullin 59a:9 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the camel chews the cud, has no upper front teeth, yet is unclean? — it has cuspid-like upper incisors: F2 — the four's exception (the camel — 101:10) against the teeth's mark; a DATA row.
- Chullin 59a:10 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the young camel without cuspids; the hyrax and hare chew with front teeth; are teeth written? — rather: whatever lacks upper incisors surely chews and parts: F2 — the mark restated as a sufficient sign; a DATA row.
- Chullin 59a:11 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. why the teeth — inspect the hooves; Rav Chisda: hooves cut in the wilderness — inspect the mouth: no upper incisors clean, provided it is not a (young) camel: F2 — the finder's fallback on one sign; a DATA row.
- Chullin 59a:12 [TOPIC] — LAW. another kind like the young camel? — the school of R. Yishmael: 'the camel … IT is unclean' (Leviticus 11:4): the Ruler of His world knows nothing else chews without parting — 'it' and no other: F2 — THE FOUR ARE THE EXCEPTION SET, CLOSED BY 'IT' (101:10's I1 row's Bavli twin — the classifier's own exception rows); the exam's row.
- Chullin 59a:13 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. Rav Chisda: the mouth mutilated — inspect the hooves: cloven clean, provided it is not a pig: F2 — the fallback's second sign; a DATA row.
- Chullin 59a:14 [TOPIC] — LAW. another kind like the pig? — 'the pig … IT is unclean' (Leviticus 11:7): nothing else parts without chewing: F2 — the swine's exclusivity, the exception set closed on its second side; the exam's row.
- Chullin 59a:15 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. mouth and hooves both gone — inspect the flesh: runs warp and woof — clean, provided it is not a wild donkey: F2 — the third fallback; a DATA row.

- Chullin 59b:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. the signs are given to PERMIT ITS FAT (forbidden in the domestic, permitted in the wild): a beast with horns and cloven hooves is wild: F2 — THE WILD BEAST'S HORN SIGNS FOR THE FAT (the design's F2 exam row) — the value; the exam's row.

- Chullin 59b:3 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the goat has horns and cloven hooves yet its fat is forbidden — layered horns required; the bull's layered — grooved required: F2 — the horn marks refined; a DATA row.
- Chullin 59b:4 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. the goat's grooved — branched required; the gazelle's not branched yet permitted — rounded, not broad like a sheep's: F2 — the marks refined again; a DATA row.

- Chullin 61a:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. as the nesher has no extra digit, no crop, an unpeelable gizzard and claws — all like it unclean; as the dove and pigeon have the four and are fit for the altar — all like them clean; Abaye: the signs' EXPLANATION is not in the Torah but learned from the Sages: F3 — THE_BIRDS_SIGNS DERIVED FROM THE TWO POLES (the nesher, the dove) and named as the Sages' teaching — THE CODE/DATA SEPARATION LAW'S OWN CASE at its folio; the exam's row.
- Chullin 61a:2 [TOPIC] — LAW. R. Chiyya: a bird with ONE clean sign and not listed as unclean is clean — unlike the nesher, which has none; the nesher written to teach exactly that: F3 — the_birds_signs' threshold ONE SIGN (R. Chiyya); the exam's row.
- Chullin 61a:3 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. why from the nesher — derive from the doves: all four signs required: F3 — the counter; a DATA row.
- Chullin 61a:4 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. if from the dove, why write the other unclean birds — none has all four: so one does not learn from the dove: F3 — the ladder's first rung; a DATA row.
- Chullin 61a:5 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. derive from the rest — three signs and still unclean, so three signs unclean, two or one all the more: F3 — the ladder; a DATA row.
- Chullin 61a:6 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. then why the crow (orev), which has only two — so the listed alone are barred, others with any signs clean: F3 — the ladder; a DATA row.
- Chullin 61b:1 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. derive from the crow — two signs unclean; then why the peres and the ozniyya (one sign each)? — even one sign clean save the listed: F3 — the ladder; a DATA row.
- Chullin 61b:2 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. derive from the peres and ozniyya — one sign unclean; then why the nesher (none)? — the nesher alone: one sign eats: F3 — the ladder's end (R. Chiyya's rule grounded); a DATA row.
- Chullin 61b:3 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. so the one-sign permission rests on the nesher's writing; but the peres and ozniyya are 'two verses that come as one' and teach no principle: F3 — the method's objection (the two-verses rule, no code in the thirteen); a DATA row.
- Chullin 61b:4 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. a tradition: the sign the peres has the ozniyya lacks and the reverse — not two as one; hence the nesher needed: F3 — the tradition on the two kinds' signs; a DATA row.

## The finds (this docket's crowns — the two runs)
- THE BIRDS' SIGNS ARE THE SAGES' TEACHING, DERIVED FROM THE TWO POLES (Chullin 61a:1 — the nesher (the great vulture) with none of the four signs, the dove with all four; Abaye: the signs' explanation is not in the Torah; the ladder 61a:2-61b:7 ending at ONE SIGN; the twenty-four unclean kinds with their signs distributed 61b:6) — THE CODE/DATA SEPARATION LAW'S OWN CASE at its folio: the_birds_signs' value in THREE LAYERS — the four signs (Mishnah Chullin 3:6 at 59a:6), the ruling "one sign and no clawing" (62a:4 Ameimar) with its knowledge condition (62a:1 Rav Nachman — who knows the twenty-four), and THE TRADITION ARM (63b:11 R. Yitzchak — a clean bird eaten by tradition, the hunter believed; Tosefta Bekhorot 1:5); the eight doubtful kinds by the gizzard's knife (62b:5); the eggs' signs NOT TORAH LAW (64a:5) and the unclean bird's egg barred from the list's own name (64b:4 — "the daughter of the ostrich").
- THE TWO LISTS COUNTED AT THEIR SEAT (Chullin 63a:17-63b:2; Bekhorot 6b:20; Chullin 63b:4): Leviticus twenty, Deuteronomy twenty-one; the da'ah and the ra'ah one bird, the ayya and the dayya one — twenty-four, both names written against the disputant (63b:3 Rabbi); R. Abbahu's twenty-three (63b:5-7); the beasts' list repeated to ADD the shesua (the cleft one — 60b:7 "was Moses a hunter?"; Niddah 24a:13), the birds' only to EXPLAIN the ra'ah: the reading's diff of 14:12-18 against Leviticus 11:13-19 confirmed by the Gemara's own arithmetic; THE COUNTING RULE (63b:9 — the smaller set is listed).
- THE FOUR CLOSED BY "IT" AND THE TEN NAMED AND NO MORE (Chullin 59a:12, 59a:14 — the school of R. Yishmael: the Ruler of His world knows no other chews without parting; 80a:13 R. Yitzchak — ten clean animals, three domestic and seven wild; 80a:14-16 — the list closed against particular-then-general (I5): "why list them all?"); the wild under the beast's name (71a:2; Zevachim 34a:15); "seh" (a lamb or kid) THE PARADIGM (I3) excluding the hybrid (Bava Kamma 77b:13, 78a:3; Chullin 78b:1); the offspring rule's dispute (Bekhorot 6b:2, 7a:7, 7a:20; Tosefta Bekhorot 1:5 — "camel, camel"; the koy (a doubtful kind) four ways at 80a:11-12).
- THE FETUS FROM 14:6'S OWN WORDS (Chullin 68a:1 the mishnah; 69a:19 "in the animal", 69a:22 "and every", 69a:23 "it" the limit, 68b:8 "hoof / hooves" for the cut's place and THE UNCLOVEN FETUS permitted inside its mother; 77a:11-15 the placenta by "every"): the limb extended and returned — Rav bars it by "flesh in the field, torn" (68a:3-68b:14, Ulla refuted), R. Yochanan permits (68b:10); THE TORN'S SLAUGHTER PURIFIES (Mishnah Chullin 4:4 at 72b:1-7 — "any carcass" the torn included, no carcass impurity); the stomach's milk a secretion, not food (116b:16).
- THE MONEY'S FORM AT THREE SEATS (Bava Metzia 47b:5 — R. Yishmael the blank bound in the hand, R. Akiva the imprint; Mishnah Eduyot 3:2 — R. Dosa / the Sages; Mishnah Maaser Sheni 1:2 — coined, current, IN ONE'S POSSESSION; Bava Kamma 98a:4 the money in the sea) with any coinage (Bekhorot 50b:5; Kiddushin 11b:3) and the coined arm applied five times outside the chapter (Berakhot 47b:10, Eruvin 31b:12, Pesachim 35b:15, Shabbat 128a:3, Bekhorot 51a:6); THE SECOND TITHE HEAVEN'S PROPERTY as ruled (Kiddushin 54b:4 — per R. Meir, from the preferred tractate; Maaser Sheni 1:1's four bars).
- THE CLASS FROM THE FOUR NAMED BY GENERAL-PARTICULAR-GENERAL (I6 — Bava Kamma 54b:20, 63a:6; NAZIR 35b:3: THE CHAPTER'S VERSE THE SOURCE OF THE METHOD; the rival amplification-restriction at Eruvin 27b:14) with water and salt out (Eruvin 26b:18; Mishnah Eruvin 3:1; Maaser Sheni 1:5, 1:7, 2:1) and the containers from the four names (Eruvin 27b:3 Ben Bag Bag; Maaser Sheni 1:3-4, 3:12-13; Chullin 1:7's unfermented drink).
- THE WALL AT THE ANSWER SHEET AND ITS TWO CAPACITIES (Maaser Sheni 3:5-7; Makkot 20a:7 Rabba — the wall for eating Torah law, for admission rabbinic; 20a:3 the third set of lashes outside; Chullin 68b:13 the tithe that left and returned permitted), THE HOUSE STANDING (Temurah 21a:17), the firstling from outside the Land (21b:3), the year passed no bar (Zevachim 29b:2), THE EXILE'S THIRD STATE ON THE TITHE (Maaser Sheni 1:5-6 — no Temple: left to rot, buried with its hide) and the liability by the house or the courtyard from 14:29's own phrase "in your gates" (Bava Metzia 88a:1-2).
- ONE TITHE NOT TWO AND THE LEVITE NEVER INTERRUPTED (Rosh Hashanah 12b:1-5 — the second year into the third; "the year of THE tithe"; R. Yehuda from 14:29's Levite, R. Eliezer ben Yaakov from the inheritance's juxtaposition), THE TITHES' NEW YEAR (12a:6-9 — Tishri 1; "tithes" plural the animal's and the grain's) and THE FIRST THIRD (12b:9-11, 13a:3 — from 31:10's Sukkot and the festival of gathering; Joshua's omer 13a:7-13); the first tithe's recipient disputed and EZRA'S PENALTY ON THE LEVITES (Yevamot 86a:1, 86a:14, 86b:4-7 — to the poor, or priests as poor); no animal tithe now by decree (Bekhorot 53b:5); the cattle tithe's answer sheet with the tenth an ordinal (Mishnah Bekhorot 9:1, 9:7).
- THE ANALOGY BOTH WAYS AT TWO SEATS (Makkot 20a:14; Kiddushin 36a:7-8 — each spot and the whole head to Israel, "for the dead" to the priests; "korcha" one word, two conclusions), THE MEASURE THREE WAYS (Makkot 20b:2-3 — the bean, visible skin, two hairs; Shabbat 94b:8), the count per soul (Makkot 21a:1, 20b:11), the condition "for the dead" (20b:10 — not a fallen house), the cutting for idolatry beside it (21a:3 — Baal's priests), Isi's persons (Kiddushin 35b:16, 36a:2-5), THE FRONTLETS' PLACE FROM THE BALDNESS (36a:10), the sonship's arms (36a:11-14 — R. Yehuda conditional, R. Meir either way by four verses), and NO FACTIONS FROM ONE WORD (Yevamot 13b:17-18 — "lo titgodedu": the cuts for the dead and the factions; 14a:6-7 — two courts in one town (Abaye), one court split (Rava); the places' customs no factions — R. Yosei HaGelili's locale ate fowl in milk, 14a:8).
- THE CARCASS DEFINED BY FITNESS FOR A RESIDENT ALIEN (Avodah Zarah 67b:8; Bekhorot 23b:2; Chullin 71a:19), the sale clause THE PARADIGM OF A PERMITTED BENEFIT (Kiddushin 56b:10; Pesachim 21b:6, 21b:10-11 — R. Abbahu's rule and R. Yehuda's torn), the sanction a dispute (Yoma 36b:1), THE TABLE'S TWO ARMS AT THEIR SEAT (Pesachim 21b:7 R. Meir both to both / 21b:8 R. Yehuda as written; 21b:9 — the resident alien SUSTAINED, not the gentile: 104:2's first person defined), THE CUSTOM'S BAR (Pesachim 50b:1, 50b:11 — the fathers' custom binds the sons; 51a:10-11 its direction, the Land over Babylonia).
- THE KID'S THREE READINGS AT THEIR SEAT (Kiddushin 57b:9 — the school of R. Yishmael: eating, benefit, cooking) with the benefit counter-arm three times (57b:10; Bekhorot 10a:2; Menachot 101b:6 — R. Shimon ben Yehuda by "holy"/"holy men"), the fowl out by "its mother's milk" (Shabbat 130a:11), the meat not the milk (Chullin 108b:2) and the Tosefta's measures and exclusions (Tosefta Chullin 8:3).
- THE POOR'S MEASURES AND THE POOR MAN'S TITHE'S USES (Mishnah Peah 8:5-6; Tosefta Peah 4:2 — "in the years of the poor man's tithe", 4:8-9 the wanderer and the thresholds, 4:10 "sufficient for his lack" — even a horse, 4:16 — no debt, no ransom, no wedding gift, no charity from it; Yevamot 86a:4 — its non-separation makes untithed produce); THE VERSE NO ONE CITES (14:9) READ THROUGH ITS TWIN (Chullin 66b:6-7 — a command and a ban; the permission's reach: vessels, pits, ditches and caves).
